- main:
  - text: Components
  - heading "Component Documentation" [level=1]
  - paragraph: Browse public JRNG components, rendered previews, code examples, API contracts, and design tokens from one addressable documentation surface.
  - link "Charts":
    - /url: /docs/charts
  - link "Getting started":
    - /url: /docs
  - main:
    - article:
      - navigation "Component documentation view":
        - button "Features" [pressed]
        - button "API"
      - navigation "Breadcrumb":
        - list:
          - listitem:
            - link "Documentation":
              - /url: /docs
          - listitem:
            - link "Components":
              - /url: /docs/components
          - listitem: Table
      - heading "Table" [level=1]
      - paragraph: Table configures bulk actions, caption, collapsible row groups, column groups, and column resize mode.
      - heading "Overview" [level=3]
      - paragraph: Table configures bulk actions, caption, collapsible row groups, column groups, and column resize mode.
      - heading "Import" [level=3]
      - text: Import TS
      - button "Copy"
      - code: "import { JTableCellTemplateDirective, JTableComponent, JTableColumn, JTableEmptyTemplateDirective, JTableHeaderTemplateDirective, JTableLoadingTemplateDirective } from 'jrng-ui/table';"
      - region "Basic preview and code":
        - heading "Basic" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Render a small fictional customer list with semantic, sortable column headers.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" caption="Recent customers" />
      - region "Typed columns preview and code":
        - heading "Typed columns" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Use JTableColumn<T> for checked fields, widths, alignment, value getters, and formatters.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" />
      - region "Header and cell templates preview and code":
        - heading "Header and cell templates" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Replace selected headers or cells while retaining the table data and interaction model.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: "<j-table [value]=\"customers\" [columns]=\"columns\"> <ng-template jTableHeader=\"status\" let-column>{{ column.header }} / owner</ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"status\" let-value=\"formattedValue\"><strong>{{ value }}</strong></ng-template> </j-table>"
      - region "Visual variants preview and code":
        - heading "Visual variants" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Choose a recognizable surface concept without changing table behavior.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" variant="gridlines" />
      - region "Density preview and code":
        - heading "Density" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Set information spacing independently from the visual variant.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" density="compact" />
      - region "Skeleton loading preview and code":
        - heading "Skeleton loading" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Reserve table space with representative rows while records load.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="[]" [columns]="columns" loading loadingVariant="skeleton" [skeletonRows]="4" />
      - region "Spinner and overlay loading preview and code":
        - heading "Spinner and overlay loading" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Keep existing rows visible when refreshing data in place.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" loading loadingVariant="overlay" />
      - region "No data preview and code":
        - heading "No data" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Explain that the source dataset has no records and optionally offer a recovery action.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="[]" [columns]="columns" emptyTitle="No customers yet" emptyDescription="New customers will appear here." emptyActionLabel="Add customer" />
      - region "No results preview and code":
        - heading "No results" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Automatically distinguish an active filter returning zero matches from an empty dataset.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" globalFilter="not-a-match" noResultsTitle="No matching customers" />
      - region "Error state preview and code":
        - heading "Error state" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Present a loading failure as an alert without treating it as ordinary emptiness.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="[]" [columns]="columns" [errorState]="loadError" emptyActionLabel="Retry" />
      - region "Selection preview and code":
        - heading "Selection" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Use the established row or checkbox selection behavior with any presentation.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" selectionMode="checkbox" />
      - region "Pagination preview and code":
        - heading "Pagination" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Page local or server-backed rows without changing empty-state semantics.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" paginator [rows]="3" />
      - region "Sorting preview and code":
        - heading "Sorting" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Mark sortable columns and activate them with pointer or keyboard input.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" sortField="outstandingBalance" [sortOrder]="-1" />
      - region "Filtering preview and code":
        - heading "Filtering" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Use the reusable filter row and typed match-mode configuration.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" filterDisplay="row" showGlobalFilter />
      - region "Tree Table preview and code":
        - heading "Tree Table" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Use the separate tree grid for hierarchical records, expansion, and node selection.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-tree-table [value]="treeNodes" [columns]="treeColumns" />
      - region "Lazy Tree Table preview and code":
        - heading "Lazy Tree Table" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Load a node’s children on demand while preserving hierarchy and focus.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-tree-table [value]="lazyTreeNodes" [columns]="treeColumns" lazy (nodeExpand)="loadChildren($event)" />
      - region "Accessibility and keyboard preview and code":
        - heading "Accessibility and keyboard" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Tables expose semantic headers and sort state; Tree Table exposes tree-grid level, selection, and expansion state.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: <j-table [value]="customers" [columns]="columns" caption="Customers awaiting review" selectionMode="checkbox" />
      - region "Composition preview and code":
        - heading "Composition" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Column metadata, empty content, and loading content are integrated Table capabilities.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: "<j-table [value]=\"customers\" [columns]=\"columns\" loadingVariant=\"skeleton\"> <ng-template jTableEmpty let-state>{{ state }}</ng-template> <ng-template jTableLoading let-variant>{{ variant }}</ng-template> </j-table>"
      - region "Inline Column Filters preview and code":
        - heading "Inline Column Filters" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Display filter controls directly below each column header for quick data filtering.
        - tablist "Example source files":

        - code: "<div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-actions\"> <p class=\"j-docs-table-filter-result\" role=\"status\"> {{ inlineState() === 'empty' ? account manager\" clearable [options]=\"managerOptions\" [ngModel]=\"value\" (ngModelChange)=\"apply($event)\" /> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableFilter=\"status\" let-value=\"value\" let-apply=\"apply\"> <j-select ariaLabel=\"Filter status\" clearable [options]=\"statusOptions\" [ngModel]=\"value\" (ngModelChange)=\"apply($event)\" /> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableFilter=\"joinedDate\" let-value=\"value\" let-apply=\"apply\"> <j-date-picker ariaLabel=\"Filter joined date\" clearable dataType=\"string\" [value]=\"$any(value)\" (valueChange)=\"apply($event)\" /> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableFilter=\"outstandingBalance\" let-value=\"value\" let-apply=\"apply\"> <j-input-number ariaLabel=\"Minimum outstanding balance\" clearable [ngModel]=\"value\" (ngModelChange)=\"apply($event)\" /> </ng-template> </j-table>"
      - region "Filters Above Table preview and code":
        - heading "Filters Above Table" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Use a dedicated filter toolbar above the table for advanced and responsive filtering.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "SCSS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: "<div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-toolbar\" aria-label=\"Customer filters\"> <j-input label=\"Search customers\" clearable [value]=\"search()\" (valueChange)=\"search.set($event)\" /> <j-select label=\"Account Manager\" clearable [options]=\"managerOptions\" [ngModel]=\"manager()\" (ngModelChange)=\"manager.set(asText($event))\" /> <j-select label=\"Industry\" clearable [options]=\"industryOptions\" [ngModel]=\"industry()\" (ngModelChange)=\"industry.set(asText($event))\" /> <j-select label=\"Subscription\" clearable [options]=\"subscriptionOptions\" [ngModel]=\"subscription()\" (ngModelChange)=\"subscription.set(asText($event))\" /> <j-select label=\"Status\" clearable [options]=\"statusOptions\" [ngModel]=\"status()\" (ngModelChange)=\"status.set(asText($event))\" /> <j-date-picker label=\"Joined after\" clearable dataType=\"string\" [value]=\"joinedFrom()\" (valueChange)=\"joinedFrom.set(asDateText($event))\" /> <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-buttons\"> <j-button label=\"Clear Filters\" variant=\"outlined\" (onClick)=\"clearAll()\" /> <j-button label=\"Apply Filters\" (onClick)=\"applyFilters()\" /> </div> </div> @if (activeChips().length) { <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-chips\" aria-label=\"Active filters\"> @for (chip of activeChips(); track chip.key) { <j-chip [label]=\"chip.label\" removable [removeAriaLabel]=\"'Remove ' + chip.label\" (remove)=\"removeFilter(chip.key)\" /> } </div> } <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-actions\"> <p class=\"j-docs-table-filter-result\" role=\"status\">{{ visibleRows().length }} customers</p> <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-buttons\"> <j-button label=\"Loading state\" variant=\"outlined\" (onClick)=\"loading.set(!loading())\" /> <j-button label=\"Empty result\" variant=\"text\" (onClick)=\"forceEmpty.set(!forceEmpty())\" /> </div> </div> <j-table caption=\"Customers filtered above the table\" [value]=\"forceEmpty() ? [] : visibleRows()\" [columns]=\"toolbarColumns\" [loading]=\"loading()\" [paginator]=\"true\" [rows]=\"5\" [scrollable]=\"true\" [tableStyle]=\"{ 'min-width': '82rem' }\" [showCurrentPageReport]=\"true\" [showGlobalFilter]=\"false\" [showColumnManager]=\"false\" [showExport]=\"false\" [maximizable]=\"false\" noResultsTitle=\"No matching customers\" > <ng-template jTableCell=\"customerName\" let-row> <span class=\"j-docs-table-customer\"> <j-avatar [initials]=\"initials($any(row)['customerName'])\" [ariaLabel]=\"$any(row)['customerName']\" size=\"sm\" /> <strong>{{ $any(row)['customerName'] }}</strong> </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"status\" let-value=\"value\"> <j-badge [value]=\"$any(value)\" [severity]=\"value === 'Active' ? 'success' : 'warning'\" variant=\"soft\" /> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"actions\" let-row> <j-button icon=\"eye\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" [ariaLabel]=\"'View ' + $any(row)['customerName']\" jTooltip=\"View customer\" /> </ng-template> </j-table>"
      - region "Expandable Filter Panel preview and code":
        - heading "Expandable Filter Panel" [level=3]
        - paragraph: Open an advanced filter panel above the table only when filtering controls are needed.
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "SCSS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: "<div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-actions\"> <j-button icon=\"filter\" [label]=\"panelOpen() ? 'Hide Filters' : 'Filters'\" [ariaLabel]=\"panelOpen() ? 'Hide customer filters' : 'Show customer filters'\" ariaControls=\"customer-advanced-filter-panel\" [ariaExpanded]=\"panelOpen()\" (onClick)=\"panelOpen.set(!panelOpen())\" > @if (activeChips().length) { <span class=\"j-docs-table-filter-count\"> <j-badge [value]=\"activeChips().length\" ariaLabel=\"Active filter count\" /> </span> } </j-button> <p class=\"j-docs-table-filter-result\" role=\"status\">{{ visibleRows().length }} customers</p> </div> <section id=\"customer-advanced-filter-panel\" class=\"j-docs-table-filter-panel\" [attr.data-open]=\"panelOpen()\" [attr.aria-hidden]=\"!panelOpen()\" > <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-panel__inner\"> <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-toolbar\"> <j-input label=\"Global search\" clearable [value]=\"search()\" (valueChange)=\"search.set($event)\" /> <j-input label=\"Customer name or email\" clearable [value]=\"customer()\" (valueChange)=\"customer.set($event)\" /> <j-select label=\"Industry\" clearable [options]=\"industryOptions\" [ngModel]=\"industry()\" (ngModelChange)=\"industry.set(asText($event))\" /> <j-select label=\"Subscription\" clearable [options]=\"subscriptionOptions\" [ngModel]=\"subscription()\" (ngModelChange)=\"subscription.set(asText($event))\" /> <j-select label=\"Status\" clearable [options]=\"statusOptions\" [ngModel]=\"status()\" (ngModelChange)=\"status.set(asText($event))\" /> <j-date-picker label=\"Joined from\" clearable dataType=\"string\" [value]=\"joinedFrom()\" (valueChange)=\"joinedFrom.set(asDateText($event))\" /> <j-date-picker label=\"Joined to\" clearable dataType=\"string\" [value]=\"joinedTo()\" (valueChange)=\"joinedTo.set(asDateText($event))\" /> <j-date-picker label=\"Last active from\" clearable dataType=\"string\" [value]=\"activeFrom()\" (valueChange)=\"activeFrom.set(asDateText($event))\" /> <j-date-picker label=\"Last active to\" clearable dataType=\"string\" [value]=\"activeTo()\" (valueChange)=\"activeTo.set(asDateText($event))\" /> <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-buttons\"> <j-button label=\"Clear All\" variant=\"outlined\" (onClick)=\"clearAll()\" /> <j-button label=\"Apply Filters\" (onClick)=\"applyFilters()\" /> </div> </div> </div> </section> @if (!panelOpen() && activeChips().length) { <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-chips\" aria-label=\"Active filters\"> @for (chip of activeChips(); track chip.key) { <j-chip [label]=\"chip.label\" removable [removeAriaLabel]=\"'Remove ' + chip.label\" (remove)=\"removeFilter(chip.key)\" /> } </div> } <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-actions\"> <span></span> <div class=\"j-docs-table-filter-buttons\"> <j-button label=\"Loading state\" variant=\"outlined\" (onClick)=\"loading.set(!loading())\" /> <j-button label=\"Empty result\" variant=\"text\" (onClick)=\"forceEmpty.set(!forceEmpty())\" /> </div> </div> <j-table caption=\"Customers with expandable advanced filters\" [value]=\"forceEmpty() ? [] : visibleRows()\" [columns]=\"toolbarColumns\" [loading]=\"loading()\" [paginator]=\"true\" [rows]=\"5\" [scrollable]=\"true\" [tableStyle]=\"{ 'min-width': '82rem' }\" [showCurrentPageReport]=\"true\" [showGlobalFilter]=\"false\" [showColumnManager]=\"false\" [showExport]=\"false\" [maximizable]=\"false\" noResultsTitle=\"No matching customers\" > <ng-template jTableCell=\"customerName\" let-row> <span class=\"j-docs-table-customer\"> <j-avatar [initials]=\"initials($any(row)['customerName'])\" [ariaLabel]=\"$any(row)['customerName']\" size=\"sm\" /> <strong>{{ $any(row)['customerName'] }}</strong> </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"status\" let-value=\"value\"> <j-badge [value]=\"$any(value)\" [severity]=\"value === 'Active' ? 'success' : 'warning'\" variant=\"soft\" /> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"actions\" let-row> <j-button icon=\"eye\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" [ariaLabel]=\"'View ' + $any(row)['customerName']\" jTooltip=\"View customer\" /> </ng-template> </j-table>"
      - region "Basic table preview and code":
        - heading "Basic table" [level=3]
        - paragraph: "Basic table using independent local state and realistic business records. Expected behavior: the visible controls update only this example and emit a polite status message. Accessibility: semantic headers, keyboard focus, labelled controls, and responsive scrolling remain available."
        - tablist "Example source files":
          - tab "HTML" [selected]
          - tab "TS"
          - tab "SCSS"
          - tab "Data"
        - button "Copy active example code": Copy
        - code: "<j-table [value]=\"scenario().includes('empty-state') || scenario().includes('loading-state') ? [] : rows.slice(0, 5)\" [columns]=\"scenario().includes('responsive') || scenario().includes('dynamic') ? wideColumns : scenario().includes('conditional-cell') ? conditionalColumns : columns\" [density]=\"scenario().includes('small') || scenario().includes('compact') ? 'compact' : scenario().includes('large') ? 'spacious' : 'comfortable'\" [variant]=\"scenario().includes('grid-lines') ? 'gridlines' : scenario().includes('striped') ? 'striped' : 'standard'\" [responsiveMode]=\"scenario().includes('responsive') ? 'stack' : 'scroll'\" [rowClass]=\"scenario().includes('conditional-row') ? rowClass : null\" [loading]=\"scenario().includes('loading-state')\" [caption]=\"scenario()\" selectionMode=\"none\" [paginator]=\"false\" [showGlobalFilter]=\"false\" [showColumnManager]=\"false\" [showExport]=\"false\" [maximizable]=\"false\"> <ng-template jTableHeader=\"customer\" let-column>{{ column.header }} / account</ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"status\" let-value=\"formattedValue\"><strong>{{ value }}</strong></ng-template> <ng-template #jTableFooter><tr><td [attr.colspan]=\"columns.length\"><strong>Recent order total</strong></td></tr></ng-template> <ng-template jTableEmpty let-state><div class=\"j-table-demo__detail\"><strong>No customers available</strong><span>State: {{ state }}</span></div></ng-template> <ng-template jTableLoading let-variant><div class=\"j-table-demo__detail\" role=\"status\">Preparing {{ variant }} rows…</div></ng-template> </j-table>"

Define a minimum width for the table or individual columns to prevent columns from becoming too narrow.</p> } <div [class.j-table-demo__flex-scroll]=\"scenario().includes('flexible')\"> <j-table [value]=\"scenario() === 'scrolling-horizontal' ? horizontalRows : rows\" [columns]=\"scenario() === 'scrolling-horizontal' ? horizontalColumns : wideColumns\" [scrollable]=\"true\" [scrollHeight]=\"scenario() === 'scrolling-horizontal' ? '' : scenario().includes('flexible') ? <ng-template #jTableCaption let-table=\"table\"> <span class=\"j-table-demo__caption\"> <span class=\"j-table-demo__primary-actions\"> <j-button label=\"Apply Leave\" icon=\"plus\" size=\"sm\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Apply leave requested.'\" /> <j-button label=\"Leave Trackers\" icon=\"eye\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"warning\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Leave trackers requested.'\" /> </span> <span class=\"j-table-demo__caption-actions\"> <j-button icon=\"sliders\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"info\" ariaLabel=\"Table config\" title=\"Table config\" (onClick)=\"table.handleToolbarAction({ key: 'columns' })\" /> <j-button icon=\"square\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"secondary\" ariaLabel=\"Maximize table\" title=\"Maximize table\" (onClick)=\"table.handleToolbarAction({ key: 'fullscreen' })\" /> <j-button icon=\"filter\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"info\" ariaLabel=\"Toggle filters\" title=\"Toggle filters\" (onClick)=\"table.handleToolbarAction({ key: 'filters' })\" /> @if (table.activeFilterItems.length) { <j-button icon=\"close\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"danger\" ariaLabel=\"Clear filters\" title=\"Clear filters\" (onClick)=\"table.resetFilters()\" /> } <j-button icon=\"refresh\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"info\" ariaLabel=\"Refresh table\" title=\"Refresh table\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Leave requests refreshed.'\" /> <j-button icon=\"download\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"success\" ariaLabel=\"Export table\" title=\"Export table\" (onClick)=\"table.exportCSV()\" /> </span> </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"employeeName\" let-row> <span class=\"j-table-demo__customer\"> <j-avatar [initials]=\"initials($any(row)['employeeName'])\" [ariaLabel]=\"$any(row)['employeeName']\" size=\"sm\" /> <span class=\"j-table-demo__customer-copy\"> <strong>{{ $any(row)['employeeName'] }}</strong> <small>{{ $any(row)['employeeCode'] }}</small> </span> </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"days\" let-value=\"formattedValue\"><j-badge [value]=\"value\" variant=\"soft\" size=\"md\" /></ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"approval\" let-row> <span class=\"j-table-demo__approval\"> @for (approver of $any(row)['approval'].split(', '); track approver) { <j-avatar [initials]=\"initials(approver)\" [ariaLabel]=\"approver\" size=\"sm\" /> } </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"status\" let-value=\"formattedValue\"> <j-badge [value]=\"value\" [severity]=\"statusSeverity(value)\" variant=\"soft\" size=\"md\" /> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableActions=\"actions\" let-row> <span class=\"j-table-demo__row-actions\"> <j-button icon=\"eye\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" variant=\"text\" [ariaLabel]=\"'View ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" title=\"View leave\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'View ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" /> @if ($any(row)['status'] !== 'Approved') { <j-button icon=\"settings\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" variant=\"text\" [ariaLabel]=\"'Approve ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" title=\"Leave approval\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Approve ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" /> <j-button icon=\"delete\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" variant=\"text\" severity=\"danger\" [ariaLabel]=\"'Delete ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" title=\"Delete leave\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Delete ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" /> } </span> </ng-template> </j-table><p class=\"j-table-demo__status\" role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\">{{ eventMessage }}</p>"

table\" (onClick)=\"table.exportCSV()\" /> </span> </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"employeeName\" let-row> <span class=\"j-table-demo__customer\"> <j-avatar [initials]=\"initials($any(row)['employeeName'])\" [ariaLabel]=\"$any(row)['employeeName']\" size=\"sm\" /> <span class=\"j-table-demo__customer-copy\"> <strong>{{ $any(row)['employeeName'] }}</strong> <small>{{ $any(row)['employeeCode'] }}</small> </span> </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"days\" let-value=\"formattedValue\"><j-badge [value]=\"value\" variant=\"soft\" size=\"md\" /></ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"approval\" let-row> <span class=\"j-table-demo__approval\"> @for (approver of $any(row)['approval'].split(', '); track approver) { <j-avatar [initials]=\"initials(approver)\" [ariaLabel]=\"approver\" size=\"sm\" /> } </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"status\" let-value=\"formattedValue\"> <j-badge [value]=\"value\" [severity]=\"statusSeverity(value)\" variant=\"soft\" size=\"md\" /> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableActions=\"actions\" let-row> <span class=\"j-table-demo__row-actions\"> <j-button icon=\"eye\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" variant=\"text\" [ariaLabel]=\"'View ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" title=\"View leave\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'View ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" /> @if ($any(row)['status'] !== 'Approved') { <j-button icon=\"settings\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" variant=\"text\" [ariaLabel]=\"'Approve ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" title=\"Leave approval\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Approve ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" /> <j-button icon=\"delete\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" variant=\"text\" severity=\"danger\" [ariaLabel]=\"'Delete ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" title=\"Delete leave\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Delete ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" /> } </span> </ng-template> </j-table><p class=\"j-table-demo__status\" role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\">{{ eventMessage }}</p>"
      - region "Admin user management preview and code":
        - heading "Admin user management" [level=3]
        - paragraph: "Admin user management using independent local state and realistic business records. Expected behavior: the visible controls update only this example and emit a polite status message. Accessibility: semantic headers, keyboard focus, labelled controls, and responsive scrolling remain available."

'min-width': '92rem' }\" [pageSize]=\"10\" [pageSizeOptions]=\"[10, 25, 50]\" selectionMode=\"none\" [showGlobalFilter]=\"false\" [showColumnManager]=\"false\" [showExport]=\"false\" [maximizable]=\"false\" [resizableColumns]=\"true\" [reorderableColumns]=\"true\" [hover]=\"true\" sortField=\"id\" [sortOrder]=\"-1\" (refresh)=\"eventMessage = 'Customer accounts refreshed.'\" > <ng-template #jTableCaption let-table=\"table\"> <span class=\"j-table-demo__caption\"> <span class=\"j-table-demo__primary-actions\"> <j-button label=\"Apply Leave\" icon=\"plus\" size=\"sm\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Apply leave requested.'\" /> <j-button label=\"Leave Trackers\" icon=\"eye\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"warning\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Leave trackers requested.'\" /> </span> <span class=\"j-table-demo__caption-actions\"> <j-button icon=\"sliders\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"info\" ariaLabel=\"Table config\" title=\"Table config\" (onClick)=\"table.handleToolbarAction({ key: 'columns' })\" /> <j-button icon=\"square\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"secondary\" ariaLabel=\"Maximize table\" title=\"Maximize table\" (onClick)=\"table.handleToolbarAction({ key: 'fullscreen' })\" /> <j-button icon=\"filter\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"info\" ariaLabel=\"Toggle filters\" title=\"Toggle filters\" (onClick)=\"table.handleToolbarAction({ key: 'filters' })\" /> @if (table.activeFilterItems.length) { <j-button icon=\"close\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"danger\" ariaLabel=\"Clear filters\" title=\"Clear filters\" (onClick)=\"table.resetFilters()\" /> } <j-button icon=\"refresh\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"info\" ariaLabel=\"Refresh table\" title=\"Refresh table\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Leave requests refreshed.'\" /> <j-button icon=\"download\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" severity=\"success\" ariaLabel=\"Export table\" title=\"Export table\" (onClick)=\"table.exportCSV()\" /> </span> </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"employeeName\" let-row> <span class=\"j-table-demo__customer\"> <j-avatar [initials]=\"initials($any(row)['employeeName'])\" [ariaLabel]=\"$any(row)['employeeName']\" size=\"sm\" /> <span class=\"j-table-demo__customer-copy\"> <strong>{{ $any(row)['employeeName'] }}</strong> <small>{{ $any(row)['employeeCode'] }}</small> </span> </span> </ng-template> <ng-template jTableCell=\"days\" let-value=\"formattedValue\"><j-badge [value]=\"value\" variant=\"soft\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" variant=\"text\" [ariaLabel]=\"'View ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" title=\"View leave\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'View ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" /> @if ($any(row)['status'] !== 'Approved') { <j-button icon=\"settings\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" variant=\"text\" [ariaLabel]=\"'Approve ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" title=\"Leave approval\" (onClick)=\"eventMessage = 'Approve ' + $any(row)['employeeName']\" /> <j-button icon=\"delete\" actionDisplay=\"icon\" size=\"sm\" variant=\"text\" severity=\"danger\" [ariaLabel]=\"'Delete ' +
